**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Account

### meGet

`account.meGet`

Get Current User. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.account.meGet({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  email: string | null,
  pending_email: string | null,
  first_name: string | null,
  last_name: string | null,
  phone: string | null,
  country: string | null,
  timezone: string | null,
  profile_photo: string | null,
  linkedin_url: string | null,
  email_verified: boolean,
  phone_verified: boolean,
  status: allowed_payment | not_allowed_payment | blocked | pending_review | permanently_deleted | russian_suspended | pending_phone_verification | null,
  created_at: string | null,
  workspace: {
    id: number,
    name: string | null,
    role: admin | member
  } | null,
  auth: {
    channel: app | extension | sheets | api | system | mcp,
    api_key_id: string | null,
    session_id: string | null
  }
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="metadata full type">

```ts
{
  timestamp: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="errors full type">

```ts

  name: VALIDATION_ERROR | UNAUTHORIZED | PAYMENT_REQUIRED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | NOT_SAVED | TIMEOUT | REQUEST_TIMEOUT | CONFLICT | GONE | LIST_TOO_LARGE | TOO_MANY_LISTS | LIMIT_REACHED | RATE_LIMIT | TOO_MANY_CONCURRENT_REQUESTS | INTERNAL_ERROR | SAVE_FAILED | EXPORT_FAILED | UPSTREAM_ERROR | UNAVAILABLE | bulk_action_running | VERIFICATION_ERROR,
```

## Billing

### creditsGet

`billing.creditsGet`

Get Credit Balances. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.billing.creditsGet({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  email_search: {
    available: number,
    allowance: number,
    additional: number,
    permanent: number
  },
  email_verification: {
    available: number,
    allowance: number,
    additional: number,
    permanent: number
  },
  phone_search: {
    available: number,
    allowance: number,
    additional: number,
    permanent: number
  },
  reset_at: string | null,
  wallet?: {
    balance: number,
    plan_credit: number,
    top_up: number,
    plan_credit_renews_at: string | null,
    auto_top_up_enabled: boolean,
    prices: {
      phone: number,
      verification: number
    },
    top_up_url: string
  }
}
```

### subscriptionGet

`billing.subscriptionGet`

Get Subscription. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.billing.subscriptionGet({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts

  status: active | pause | disabled,
  plan: {
    id: string,
    name: string | null,
    price: number | null,
    currency: string | null,
    is_custom: boolean,
    credits: {
      email_search: number,
      email_verification: number,
      phone_search: number

    percent: number,
```

### usageSummaryGet

`billing.usageSummaryGet`

Get Usage Summary. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.billing.usageSummaryGet({
	query: {
		from: '2026-01-01',
		to: '2026-01-01',
		users: '64f1a2b3c4d5e6f7a8b9c0d1',
		channel: 'app',
		action: 'search',
		entity: 'people',
		group_by: 'day',
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  from: string,
  to: string,
  users?: string,
  channel?: app | extension | sheets | api | mcp,
  action?: search | enrich | enrichment | save | export | csv_enrichment | email_verify | other,
  entity?: people | companies,
  group_by: day | source
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts

  totals: {
    email_search: number,
    email_verification: number,
    phone_search: number,
    email_credits: number,
    verification_credits: number,
    phone_credits: number,
    searches: number,
    enriches: number,
    people_searches: number,
    company_searches: number,
    people_enriches: number,
    company_enriches: number,

    key: string,
```

## Blocklist

### companiesAdd

`blocklist.companiesAdd`

Block Companies. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.blocklist.companiesAdd({
	body: {
		data: [{ domain: 'x' }],
	},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
{
  data: {
    domain: string,
    identifier?: string
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>

## Email

### find

`email.find`

Find Email. Credit cost: served_address (a valid address is served that the workspace does not already own (the email of a revealed or saved person, and an address charged before, are free)).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.email.find({
	body: {
		data: {},
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: served_address; a valid address is served that the workspace does not already own (the email of a revealed or saved person, and an address charged before, are free). Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request. This synchronous endpoint can take up to 95 seconds; the client uses the documented timeout.

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
    full_name?: string,
    first_name?: string,
    last_name?: string,
    domain?: string,
    company?: string
  }
}
```

### verificationsCreate

`email.verificationsCreate`

Create Email Verification. Credit cost: billable_row (when a `data.emails` job starts on its own after counting, for `counts.billable`; `unknown` rows and rows never verified are refunded when the job ends (a `file_key` job charges at `email_verifications_start`)).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.email.verificationsCreate({
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: {
		data: {},
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: billable_row; when a `data.emails` job starts on its own after counting, for `counts.billable`; `unknown` rows and rows never verified are refunded when the job ends (a `file_key` job charges at `email_verifications_start`). Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

### verificationsStart

### verify

`email.verify`

Verify Email. Credit cost: address (a valid, invalid or accept_all verdict, once per address per workspace).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.email.verify({
	body: {
		data: { email: 'person@samplemail.test' },
	},
});
```

| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

`enrichments.peopleEnrichmentsCreate`

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.enrichments.peopleEnrichmentsCreate({
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: {
		data: [{}],
	},
});
```

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
}
```

### peopleExport

`exports.peopleExport`

Export People. Credit cost: revealed profile not already owned (per chunk during the run, against the balance read at that chunk; emails past it are withheld).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.exports.peopleExport({
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: revealed profile not already owned; per chunk during the run, against the balance read at that chunk; emails past it are withheld. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

```ts
  idempotencyKey?: string
}
```

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  ids?: string[],
  filters?: {
    person?: {
```

## Jobs

### get

`jobs.get`

Get a Job. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.jobs.get({
	path: { id: '64f1a2b3c4d5e6f7a8b9c0d1' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```

## Lists

### companiesListsRemove

`lists.companiesListsRemove`

Remove Companies from List. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.lists.companiesListsRemove({
	body: {
		list_id: 'x',
		ids: ['x'],
	},
});
```

| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

### companiesListsSet

`lists.companiesListsSet`

Set Company Lists. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.lists.companiesListsSet({
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: {
		list_ids: ['x'],
	},
});
```

## People

### bulkEnrich

`people.bulkEnrich`

People Bulk Enrich. Credit cost: newly revealed email; phone number found (enrich_email (on by default) / enrich_phone, per item).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.bulkEnrich({
	body: {
		data: [{}],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: newly revealed email; phone number found; enrich_email (on by default) / enrich_phone, per item. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

```ts
  data: {
    identifier?: string,
    id?: string,
    linkedin_id?: string | number,
    linkedin_url?: string,
    email?: string,
    first_name?: string,
    last_name?: string,
    company_domain?: string,
    company_name?: string
  }[],
  enrich_email?: boolean,
  enrich_phone?: boolean
}
```

### count

`people.count`

People Count. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.count({
	body: {
		filters: {},
	},
});
```

| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

### enrich

`people.enrich`

People Enrich. Credit cost: newly revealed email; phone number found (enrich_email (on by default) / enrich_phone).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.enrich({
	body: {
		data: {},
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: newly revealed email; phone number found; enrich_email (on by default) / enrich_phone. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request. This synchronous endpoint can take up to 95 seconds; a searching email result can be polled by calling the endpoint again after retry_after.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  data: {
    id?: string,
    linkedin_id?: string | number,
    linkedin_url?: string,
    email?: string,
    first_name?: string,
    last_name?: string,
    company_domain?: string,
    company_name?: string
  },
  enrich_email?: boolean,
  enrich_phone?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

### enrichSearch

`people.enrichSearch`

Enrich Mobiles for a Search. Credit cost: phone number found (always, until the balance runs out).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.enrichSearch({
	body: {
		filters: {},
	},
});
```

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: phone number found; always, until the balance runs out. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

```ts
  filters: {
    person?: {
      id?: {
        include?: string[],
        exclude?: string[]
      },
      name?: {
        include?: string[],
        exclude?: string[]
      },
      linkedin_url?: {
        include?: string[]
      }
    },
    person_location?: {
      include?: string[],
      exclude?: string[]
    },
```

### get

`people.get`

Get a Person. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.get({
	path: { id: '64f1a2b3c4d5e6f7a8b9c0d1' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `undefined` | No | — |

### save

`people.save`

Save People. Credit cost: person whose valid email is handed over and was not already owned (always; a re-save of a person already paid for is free. A person answered `searching` costs nothing until the refreshed search finds an email and the balance allows it).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.save({
	body: {
		data: ['x'],
		list_ids: ['x'],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: person whose valid email is handed over and was not already owned; always; a re-save of a person already paid for is free. A person answered `searching` costs nothing until the refreshed search finds an email and the balance allows it. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  data: (string | {
    identifier?: string,
    id?: string,
    linkedin_url?: string,
    primary_position_id?: string,
    primary_position_index?: number,
    primary_company_id?: string
  })[],
  list_ids: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
  saved: {
    identifier: string,
    id: string,
    matched_by: string,
    list_ids: string[],

      status: string | null,
      address: string,
      revealed: true
    } | {
      status: searching,
      address: string | null,
      revealed: false,
      retry_after?: number
    } | null
  }[],
  not_matched: {
    identifier: string,
    attempted: string[]
  }[],
  invalid: {
    identifier: string,
    reason: string,
    skipped?: string[],
    unknown_fields?: string[]
  }[],
  failed: {
    identifier: string,
    id: string,
    reason: string
  }[]
}
```

### saveSearch

`people.saveSearch`

Save a Whole Search. Credit cost: person whose stored valid email is handed over and was not already owned (always, in search order, until the balance runs out).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.saveSearch({
	body: {
		filters: {},
	},
});
```

| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: person whose stored valid email is handed over and was not already owned; always, in search order, until the balance runs out. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

---

### search

`people.search`

People Search. Credit cost: newly revealed email; phone number found (only with enrich_email / enrich_phone).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.people.search({
	body: {
		filters: {},
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Credit-aware operation: newly revealed email; phone number found; only with enrich_email / enrich_phone. Check the workspace balance and the provider response before repeating the request.

### suggest

`search.suggest`

Search Suggestions. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.search.suggest({
	body: { type: 'location' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  type: location | job_title | industry | company_keywords | person | company | technology,
  query?: string,
  limit?: number,
  ids?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |

### companiesAdd

Delete Companies to Trash. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: {},
});
```

### list

`trash.list`

List Trash. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts

	query: {
		kind: 'person',
		search: 'x',
		since: 'x',
		page: 1,
		per_page: 1,
	},
});
```

### restore

`trash.restore`

Restore from Trash. Credit cost: none.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.getprospect.api.trash.restore({
	headers: { idempotencyKey: 'stable-test-key' },
	body: { kind: 'person' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `query` | `undefined` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  idempotencyKey?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  kind: person | company | list,
  ids?: string[],
  all?: boolean,
  search?: string,
  since?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `object[]` | No | — |
